- **seeflow-pr-flow-writer**: Use when the PR-review skill needs one slice of an already-authored review model turned into one registered seeflow flow — geometry, nodes, connectors, and links written through the CLI in as few `flow:add-bulk` calls as the caps allow. ([jbrinkman/kairon](https://github.com/jbrinkman/kairon/blob/HEAD/internal/eval/testdata/checks-only-invalid/agents/selftest-checks-prompt.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **selftest-fail-prompt**: You are a minimal agent used by the Kairon eval self-test. Its cases are expected to fail: each `check-*` case carries a pass/fail check that cannot pass (`check-partial` and `check-judge-list` pass one check and fail the other), and `stub-timeout` fails with a timeout. This agent exists so the `selftest` agent can keep meaning "everything passes".
